118. A few of the pieces I listed in the “ [Blurred Lines](https://lukemuehlhauser.com/how-to-fall-in-love-with-modern-classical-music-4/#alt-classical)” section of my beginner’s guide to contemporary classical music probably count as third stream, but I’m going to cover the genre more thoroughly here in my jazz guide. \[ [↩](https://lukemuehlhauser.com/a-beginners-guide-to-modern-art-jazz/#identifier_118_1336)\]
119. I considered including Mingus’ _[Jazzical Moods](https://open.spotify.com/album/6WpgYIlhjLk4qnehYwKAOp)_ (1955), but I don’t think is classical enough to be called third stream\*. I made the same call for Davis’ _[Sketches of Spain](https://open.spotify.com/album/4Gg3siRefHW8r2CndJxIiJ)_ (1960). Examples of third stream\* music that weren’t accessible enough for this list include: Lateef’s _[African-American Epic Suite](https://open.spotify.com/album/1yKjaReDDKGOB6AZi9g31O)_ (rec. 1993, rel. 1996), Weber’s _[The Colours of Chloë](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Colours_of_Chlo%C3%AB)_ (1974), Coleman’s _[Skies of America](https://open.spotify.com/album/71ZTPSnaNizoaYB6JaISqg)_ (1972), Giuffre’s _[Piece For Clarinet And String Orchestra](http://www.discogs.com/Jimmy-Giuffre-Piece-For-Clarinet-And-String-OrchestraMobiles/release/4717571)_ (1960), Alice Coltrane’s _[Lord of Lords](https://open.spotify.com/user/lukeprog/playlist/1ucwXw0EqwDxfq1CaaUAXt)_ (1972) and _[World Galaxy](https://open.spotify.com/album/4vJH84ddOAuXvRhtS0ePex)_ (1972), Macero’s _[What’s New?](https://open.spotify.com/album/0lsJVb3AeyV3VFLua2lCJj)_ (1956), Mann’s _[Concerto Grosso in D Blues](https://open.spotify.com/album/10K3D3QU0G7qvWxgb7uROM)_ (1968), Penderecki’s _[Actions for Free Jazz Orchestra](https://open.spotify.com/track/2DCWFHCJXvu9KwVuytlZtu)_ (1971), Horn’s _[Jazz Suite on the Mass Texts](http://www.allmusic.com/album/jazz-suite-on-the-mass-texts-mw0000873457)_ (1965), and Burton’s _[Seven Songs for Quartet and Chamber Orchestra](http://www.allmusic.com/album/seven-songs-for-quartet-and-chamber-orchestra-mw0000876262)_ (1973). \[ [↩](https://lukemuehlhauser.com/a-beginners-guide-to-modern-art-jazz/#identifier_119_1336)\]
120. Stan Kenton’s early albums — _[Stan Kenton Presents](https://open.spotify.com/user/lukeprog/playlist/3epR8zPN2qgTUUwiogCt0k)_ (1950), _[Innovations in Modern Music](https://open.spotify.com/user/lukeprog/playlist/500C0vQi5grqmtDGdR9Nis)_ (1950), **_[City of Glass](https://open.spotify.com/user/lukeprog/playlist/3KxTabspMf074aaevkgiY0)_** (1952), _[New Concepts in Artistry of Rhythm](https://open.spotify.com/album/1GBiPdt4ZSuqMIBLCBpdPQ)_ (1952), _[This Modern World](http://www.discogs.com/Stan-Kenton-This-Modern-World/release/3631294)_ (1953) — are definitely third stream\*, but they generally aren’t accessible enough for this list, which is why I selected particular tracks from them instead. Kenton’s _[New Concepts of Artistry in Rhythm](https://open.spotify.com/album/1GBiPdt4ZSuqMIBLCBpdPQ)_ (1952) and several later albums are accessible enough, but not mostly third stream\*. Kenton returned to third stream\* music later, e.g. on _[Conducts the Los Angeles Neophonic Orchestra](http://www.allmusic.com/album/conducts-the-los-angeles-neophonic-orchestra-mw0000040734)_ (1965), but I decided these albums weren’t reliably accessible enough for this list. \[ [↩](https://lukemuehlhauser.com/a-beginners-guide-to-modern-art-jazz/#identifier_120_1336)\]
121. Most of _[Concorde](https://open.spotify.com/album/05Xl8SCySp3AD6TeLHlSKW)_ (1955) and _[Fontessa](https://open.spotify.com/album/4ceTTa0JKnKsppT43oe8dc)_ (1956) isn’t third stream\*, so I included only the most third stream\* track from those albums, “Fontessa.” \[ [↩](https://lukemuehlhauser.com/a-beginners-guide-to-modern-art-jazz/#identifier_121_1336)\]
122. This was the most accessible-sounding piece by Schuller I could find. He wrote a lot of third stream\* music, but almost none of it is accessible enough for this list. \[ [↩](https://lukemuehlhauser.com/a-beginners-guide-to-modern-art-jazz/#identifier_122_1336)\]
123. My judgment is that Moondog’s earlier albums are not very accessible, and rarely third stream\*. \[ [↩](https://lukemuehlhauser.com/a-beginners-guide-to-modern-art-jazz/#identifier_123_1336)\]
124. I decided **_[Free Jazz](https://open.spotify.com/album/7zE4RadAWa8lYSvAZkGtFw)_** (1960), _[This Is Our Music](https://open.spotify.com/album/5dx51fo9eKRUo0GyZSKaxy)_ (1961), **_[Ornette!](https://open.spotify.com/album/7BxOi2aKqOyAcOXo6EpIFl)_** (1961), _[Chappaqua Suite](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chappaqua_Suite)_ (1965), _[Who’s Crazy](http://www.allmusic.com/album/whos-crazy-mw0000897452)_ (1966), _[Ornette at 12](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ornette_at_12)_ (1968), and _[Science Fiction](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Science_Fiction_(Ornette_Coleman_album))_ (1972) weren’t accessible enough for this section. _[Skies of America](https://open.spotify.com/album/71ZTPSnaNizoaYB6JaISqg)_ (1972) is third stream\*. [_New York is Now!_](https://open.spotify.com/album/5YExsVX6QRDHsLJLLm4ogy) (1968) and _[Love Call](https://open.spotify.com/album/2wEsCzR2h5wqur4XvHgj46)_ (1968) are accessible enough, but not very “free.” Most of Coleman’s later work is jazz-rock or jazz-funk. \[ [↩](https://lukemuehlhauser.com/a-beginners-guide-to-modern-art-jazz/#identifier_124_1336)\]
125. Many, many good albums of free jazz were excluded from this list due to insufficient accessibility, including: John Coltrane’s **_[Ascension](https://open.spotify.com/album/2zXT7xHaJ2BkzBhPtfYNhz)_** (1965), _[Sun Ship](https://open.spotify.com/album/3AGtMJ4ScmH500cQwIvIlB)_ (rec. 1965, rel. 1971), _[Om](https://open.spotify.com/album/34KLU47PjzsivJmvYJCAPM)_ (1965), and others; Albert Ayler’s **_[Spirits](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spirits_(Albert_Ayler_album))_** (rec. 1964, rel. 1966), _[New York Eye and Ear Control](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_York_Eye_and_Ear_Control)_ (1965), **_[Spiritual Unity](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spiritual_Unity)_** (1965), [_Ghosts_](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ghosts_(Albert_Ayler_album)) (1965), _[Bells](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bells_(album))_ (1965), _[Spirits Rejoice](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spirits_Rejoice)_ (1965), _[Love Cry](https://open.spotify.com/album/1g8U1UDK7L1L7sxgUngd9q)_ (1968), _[Music is the Healing Force of the Universe](https://open.spotify.com/album/6AVZC7xDWvVShSPoGrma5E)_ (1969); Sun Ra’s _Heliocentric Worlds [Vol 1](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Heliocentric_Worlds_of_Sun_Ra,_Volume_One)_ (1965), _[Vol 2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Heliocentric_Worlds_of_Sun_Ra,_Volume_Two)_ (1966), & _[Vol 3](http://www.allmusic.com/album/heliocentric-worlds-of-sun-ra-vol-3-mw0000702222)_ (rec. 1965, rel. 2005), **_[The Magic City](https://open.spotify.com/album/4RqGxTKGVcfCgMG2dSLscf)_** (1966), **_[Atlantis](https://open.spotify.com/album/7CJ5gZJKSazFiCfLmTH5l4)_** (1969), _When Angels Speak of Love_, _Featuring Pharoah Sanders And Black Harold_; Sam Rivers’ **_[Dimensions & Extensions](https://open.spotify.com/album/0tuzyiPcxxJWTtOviwqAv0)_** (rec. 1967, rel. 1986), **_[Streams](https://open.spotify.com/album/1DngvLMEYczGRUdTJyuVSx)_** (1973), **_[Crystals](https://open.spotify.com/album/6wcNGsQ4vN5gPD8jHZFWBo)_** (1974), _[The Quest](https://open.spotify.com/album/5eb5FoGfCwJb512k7UfOF3)_ (1976), _[Paragon](https://open.spotify.com/album/7sgA7KGzp2V9ePwYwshu0m)_ (1977), _[Waves](https://open.spotify.com/album/69cDVoBsZq57aVJWcqnH3r)_ (1978), and _[Tangens](https://open.spotify.com/album/5GJSEJlwsDZMsGMrqBmkZN)_ (1997); Marion Brown’s **_[Marion Brown Quartet](http://www.allmusic.com/album/the-marion-brown-quartet-mw0000095221)_** (1966), _[Juba-Lee](http://www.allmusic.com/album/juba-lee-mw0001881322)_ (1966), _[Three for Shepp](https://open.spotify.com/album/28dCpXQitZJotEUame89Ob)_ (1966), _[Porto Novo](https://open.spotify.com/album/6BSYAbfsvfdIpQBnMJYXMl)_ (1968), _Duets_, _Why Not?_; Archie Shepp’s _[Fire Music](https://open.spotify.com/album/1vz97Cr9Jw5A4tJnA7dLNk)_ (1965), [_On This Night_](https://open.spotify.com/album/637UaZxeTffGluVJMOvBS3) (1966), **_[Mama Too Tight](https://open.spotify.com/album/3qr5zSyMn6r7chKsSMZRag)_** (1966), _[The Way Ahead](https://open.spotify.com/album/2SCqHqo5CDNjbGMiL6OUKa)_ (1969), and _[Blasé](https://open.spotify.com/album/2wA29X1CXg2BI1RZiplmB2)_ (1969); Alice Coltrane’s _[Universal Consciousness](https://open.spotify.com/album/2YlrEXJnxx7q1wX8sVXIuV)_ (1971); Dave Burrell’s _[Echo](http://www.allmusic.com/album/echo-mw0000163000)_ (1969), _[After Love](https://open.spotify.com/album/2F6r6L2jNJH9sOpexCesIB)_ (1970), _High Won-High Two_, _La Vie de Boheme_, _Windward Passages \[1979\]_; Bill Dixon’s **_[Intents and Purposes](https://open.spotify.com/album/6KSjRZ3GfHWFJy98L2lFQ8)_** (1967), _[Opium](http://www.allmusic.com/album/opium-mw0000001630)_ (rec. 1969, rel. 2001), **_[Vade Mecum](https://open.spotify.com/album/5TdtEOYLmzsyPp0HLSUjuq)_** (1994), _[Vade Mecum II](https://open.spotify.com/album/2nrwIiAanqpgV5X6aQCWGd)_ (1996), _[The Enchanted Messenger](https://open.spotify.com/album/4aBWmln96wqzvRFld82tam)_ (1994), _[Considerations 1](http://www.allmusic.com/album/considerations-1-1972-1976-mw0000963038)_ (rec. 1976, rel. 1981), _[Considerations 2](http://www.allmusic.com/album/considerations-2-1972-1976-mw0001478922)_ (rec. 1976, rel. 1980), _[Collection](http://www.allmusic.com/album/collection-mw0000434340)_ (rec. 1976, rel. 1999), and _[Berlin Abbozzi](http://www.allmusic.com/album/berlin-abbozzi-mw0000325215)_ (2000); 258. As always, different critics and historians put different boundaries around their genre terms. Some people would count e.g. Paul Bley’s (really, Carla Bley’s) _[Closer](http://www.allmusic.com/album/closer-mw0000586281)_ (1965) as ECM style jazz. For my own convenience, I’m going to hue pretty close to RYM’s definition of ECM style jazz. \[ [↩](https://lukemuehlhauser.com/a-beginners-guide-to-modern-art-jazz/#identifier_258_1336)\]
